class Parser(ABC, Generic[ParserConfigT, SchemaFeaturesT])
  @property @abstractmethod def schema_features(self) -> SchemaFeaturesT
  @property def run_context(self) -> ParserRunContext
  def configure_run_context(self, *, diagnostic_source_path: Path | None=None, prefetched_source: tuple[Path, bytes] | None=None, formatter_cwd: Path | None=None, preserve_circular_root_models: bool=False, suppress_parse_warnings: bool=False, untyped_nullable_maps: bool=False, yielding_names: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, str | None]], ...]=(), document_roles: DocumentRoles=(), read_yaml_numbers_where_strings_required: bool=False, model_name_keys: ModelNameKeys | None=None, records_model_names: bool=False, final_name_keys: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], ...]=(), import_cycle_seeds: ImportCycleSeeds | None=None, planned_names: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, str | None]], ...]=()) -> None
  @property def document_roles_to_seed(self) -> DocumentRoles
  def __init__(self, source: str | Path | list[Path] | ParseResult | dict[str, YamlValue], *, config: ParserConfigT | None=None, **options: Unpack[ParserConfigDict]) -> None
  @property def field_name_model_type(self) -> ModelType
  def get_serialization_alias(self, original_field_name: str, field_name: str, class_name: str | None=None, *, output_names: tuple[str, ...]=()) -> str | None
  @property def iter_source(self) -> Iterator[Source]
  @classmethod def get_url_path_parts(cls, url: ParseResult) -> list[str]
  @property def data_type(self) -> type[DataType]
  @abstractmethod def parse_raw(self) -> None
  def record_dropped_constraints(self) -> None
  def dispose(self) -> None
  def parse(self, with_import: bool | None=True, format_: bool | None=True, settings_path: Path | None=None, disable_future_imports: bool=False, all_exports_scope: AllExportsScope | None=None, all_exports_collision_strategy: AllExportsCollisionStrategy | None=None, module_split_mode: ModuleSplitMode | None=None, collect_model_metadata: bool=False) -> str | dict[tuple[str, ...], Result]

class JsonSchemaParser(Parser['JSONSchemaParserConfig', 'JsonSchemaFeatures'])
  @snooper_to_methods()
  SCHEMA_PATHS: ClassVar[list[str]] = list(_DEFAULT_SCHEMA_PATHS)
  SCHEMA_OBJECT_TYPE: ClassVar[type[JsonSchemaObject]] = JsonSchemaObject
  REQUIRED_ONLY_SCHEMA_ALLOWED_FIELDS: ClassVar[frozenset[str]] = frozenset({'required', 'type', 'extras'})
  CONDITIONAL_SCHEMA_ALLOWED_FIELDS: ClassVar[frozenset[str]] = REQUIRED_ONLY_SCHEMA_ALLOWED_FIELDS | {'properties'}
  STRING_PROPERTY_NAME_FIELDS: ClassVar[frozenset[str]] = frozenset({'type', 'anyOf', 'pattern', 'minLength', 'maxLength', 'title', 'description', 'default'})
  COMPATIBLE_PYTHON_TYPES: ClassVar[dict[str, frozenset[str]]] = {'string': frozenset({'str', 'String'}), 'integer': frozenset({'int', 'Integer'}), 'number': frozenset({'float', 'int', 'Number'}), 'boolean': frozenset({'bool', 'Boolean'}), 'array': frozenset((name for name, given in GIVEN_CONTAINERS.items() if given.slot in ITEM_CONTAINERS.slots)), 'object': frozenset({*(name for name, given in GIVEN_CONTAINERS.items() if given.slot in MAPPINGS.slots), 'TypedDict'})}
  PYTHON_TYPE_OVERRIDE_ALWAYS: ClassVar[frozenset[str]] = frozenset({'Callable', 'Type', 'defaultdict', 'OrderedDict', 'Counter', 'deque', 'ChainMap'})
  def __init__(self, source: str | Path | list[Path] | ParseResult, *, config: JSONSchemaParserConfig | None=None, **options: Unpack[JSONSchemaParserConfigDict]) -> None
  def get_field_extras(self, obj: JsonSchemaObject) -> dict[str, Any]
  @cached_property def schema_paths(self) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]
  @cached_property def schema_features(self) -> JsonSchemaFeatures
  @property def root_id(self) -> str | None
  @root_id.setter def root_id(self, value: str | None) -> None
  def should_parse_enum_as_literal(self, obj: JsonSchemaObject, property_name: str | None=None, property_obj: JsonSchemaObject | None=None) -> bool
  def is_constraints_field(self, obj: JsonSchemaObject, data_type: DataType | None=None) -> bool

  def parse_object_fields(self, obj: JsonSchemaObject, path: list[str], module_name: Optional[str]=None, class_name: Optional[str]=None, *, output_names: tuple[str, ...]=()) -> list[DataModelFieldBase]

__all__ = ['DataFormatMapping', 'DocumentDialect', 'FeatureMetadata', 'JsonSchemaFeatures', 'OpenAPISchemaFeatures', 'SchemaFeaturesT', 'SchemaKind', 'detect_asyncapi_version', 'detect_jsonschema_version', 'detect_openapi_version', 'document_dialect', 'get_data_formats']
class AsyncAPIClientFeatures()
  @dataclass(frozen=True)
  server_protocols: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='server protocols', description='The protocol of a server; the client opens the channels of `ws` and `wss` servers as WebSockets, and the server-sent event channels of `http`, `https` and `sse` servers', client_status='partial')
  server_sent_events: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='server-sent event channels', description="A channel that carries `text/event-stream` messages, its own or a trait's, or that no `ws` or `wss` server serves and an `sse` server serves or whose `http` binding fixes `Accept` or `Content-Type` to `text/event-stream`; the client opens it with a GET, whose query and header parameters are those of the `http` bindings of the channel and its operations, and receives the JSON data of each of its messages until the stream ends, without reconnecting; it does not receive other content types on it or open it with another method", client_status='partial')
  server_variables: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='server variables', description="The variables of a server's URL; the client substitutes each by its `default`, which OpenAPI requires and AsyncAPI does not", client_status='partial')
  channel_addresses: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 3.0', doc_name='channels without an address', description='A channel whose `address` is null or absent, which the client cannot open', client_status='not_supported')
  websocket_bindings: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='WebSocket channel bindings', description='`bindings.ws` of a channel; the client opens GET handshakes, with the properties of objects `query` and `headers` as parameters, and the values a `Sec-WebSocket-Protocol` header fixes as the subprotocols it offers', client_status='partial')
  message_content_types: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='message content types', description='The payloads of messages of AsyncAPI, JSON Schema or OpenAPI schemas; the client codes JSON by the models, binary media and binary strings as bytes, and text media whose models type a string as UTF-8 text; a form or multipart message is carried as bytes where its payload is a binary string, and not where it is structured', client_status='partial')
  message_payloads: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='messages without a payload', description='A message that declares no `payload`, which the client cannot code', client_status='not_supported')
  message_headers: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='message headers', description='The `headers` of a message; the client codes its payload but neither sends nor reads headers, which a WebSocket frame does not carry, and opens the channel without a warning', client_status='partial')
  several_messages: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='several messages per direction', description='More than one message that an application sends, or receives, on one channel; the client tells JSON messages apart by a tag their models declare, else by their class when it sends them, and Pydantic models code them as a union; it does not receive text and JSON messages on one channel', client_status='partial')
  shared_addresses: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='channels sharing an address', description='Channels at one address on the same servers, which the client opens as one connection that carries the messages of them all; their handshakes must take the same parameters, offer the same subprotocols and need the same security', client_status='partial')
  operation_replies: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 3.0', doc_name='operation replies', description="The `reply` of an operation; the client sends or receives its messages on the operation's connection, without correlating them with a request, and does not open a reply on a channel at another address or sent to an address a runtime expression gives", client_status='partial')
  security_requirements: bool = _client_feature(introduced='AsyncAPI 2.0', doc_name='security requirements', description="The security of a server or an operation, its own or a trait's; the client sends `httpApiKey`, `http`, `oauth2` and `openIdConnect` credentials on a handshake, and needs every server of a channel to require the same", client_status='partial')

class JsonSchemaFeatures()
  @dataclass(frozen=True)
  null_in_type_array: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 4 or earlier', doc_name='Null in type array', description="Allows `type: ['string', 'null']` syntax for nullable types", status='supported'))
  defs_not_definitions: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='$defs', description='Uses `$defs` instead of `definitions` for schema definitions', status='supported'))
  prefix_items: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2020-12', doc_name='prefixItems', description='Tuple validation using `prefixItems` keyword', status='supported'))
  boolean_schemas: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 6', doc_name='Boolean schemas', description='Allows `true` and `false` as valid schemas', status='supported'))
  id_field: str = field(default='$id', metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 6', doc_name='$id', description='Schema identifier field (`id` in Draft 4, `$id` in Draft 6+)', status='supported'))
  definitions_key: str = field(default='$defs', metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 4', doc_name='definitions/$defs', description='Key for reusable schema definitions', status='supported'))
  exclusive_as_number: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 6', doc_name='exclusiveMinimum/Maximum as number', description='Numeric `exclusiveMinimum`/`exclusiveMaximum` (boolean in Draft 4)', status='supported'))
  read_only_write_only: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 7', doc_name='readOnly/writeOnly', description='Field visibility hints for read-only and write-only properties', status='supported'))
  const_support: bool = field(default=True, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 6', doc_name='const', description='Single constant value constraint', status='supported'))
  property_names: bool = field(default=True, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 6', doc_name='propertyNames', description='Dict key type constraints via pattern, enum, or $ref', status='supported'))
  contains: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 6', doc_name='contains', description='Count constraints are modeled when contains matches every item; general schema-valued contains is not supported', status='partial'))
  contains_counts: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='minContains/maxContains', description='Count limits on the items `contains` matches (a `contains` needs one match before 2019-09)', status='partial'))
  deprecated_keyword: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='deprecated', description='Marks schema elements as deprecated', status='partial'))
  if_then_else: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 7', doc_name='if/then/else', description='Conditional schema validation', status='not_supported'))
  content_encoding: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='Draft 7', doc_name='contentMediaType/contentEncoding', description='Content type and encoding hints for strings', status='partial'))
  content_schema: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='contentSchema', description='Schema for decoded string content', status='partial'))
  anchor: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='$anchor', description='Location-independent schema references', status='supported'))
  vocabulary: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='$vocabulary', description='Vocabulary declarations for meta-schemas', status='not_supported'))
  unevaluated_properties: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='unevaluatedProperties', description='Additional properties not evaluated by subschemas', status='partial'))
  unevaluated_items: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='unevaluatedItems', description='Additional items not evaluated by subschemas', status='partial'))
  dependent_required: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='dependentRequired', description='Conditional property requirements', status='not_supported'))
  dependent_schemas: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='dependentSchemas', description='Conditional schema application based on property presence', status='not_supported'))
  recursive_ref: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='$recursiveRef/$recursiveAnchor', description='Recursive reference resolution via anchors', status='supported'))
  dynamic_ref: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2020-12', doc_name='$dynamicRef/$dynamicAnchor', description='Dynamic reference resolution across schemas', status='supported'))
  ref_sibling_keywords: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='2019-09', doc_name='$ref with sibling keywords', description='Keywords beside `$ref` apply with the referenced schema (ignored in Draft 4-7 and OAS 3.0)', status='partial'))
  @classmethod def from_version(cls, version: JsonSchemaVersion) -> JsonSchemaFeatures
JsonSchemaVersion = import datamodel_code_generator.enums.JsonSchemaVersion
class OpenAPISchemaFeatures(JsonSchemaFeatures)
  @dataclass(frozen=True)
  nullable_keyword: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='nullable', description='Uses `nullable: true` for nullable types (deprecated in 3.1)', status='supported'))
  discriminator_support: bool = field(default=True, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='discriminator', description='Polymorphism support via `discriminator` keyword', status='supported'))
  webhooks: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.1', doc_name='webhooks', description='Top-level webhooks object for incoming events; the client generates no methods for webhook operations, only helpers that verify and decode deliveries', status='supported', client_status='partial'))
  media_item_schema: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.2', doc_name='itemSchema', description='Media Type Object item schema for sequential media', status='supported'))
  openapi_self: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.2', doc_name='$self', description='Root document URI for relative and absolute reference resolution', status='supported'))
  querystring_parameter: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.2', doc_name='querystring', description='Query string parameter object without a parameter name; the client ignores the `encoding` declarations of the members of a nameless form querystring', status='supported', client_status='partial'))
  xml_support: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='xml', description='XML serialization metadata (name, namespace, prefix)', status='partial'))
  external_docs: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='externalDocs', description='Reference to external documentation', status='partial'))
  links: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='links', description='Links between operations', status='not_supported'))
  callbacks: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='callbacks', description='Callback definitions for webhooks', status='not_supported'))
  security_schemes: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='securitySchemes', description='API security mechanism definitions', status='not_supported'))
  http_auth_schemes: bool = field(default=True, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='http security schemes', description='HTTP authentication schemes; the client sends `basic` and `bearer` only', status='not_supported', client_status='partial'))
  mutual_tls: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.1', doc_name='mutualTLS', description='Mutual TLS security scheme', status='not_supported', client_status='not_supported'))
  cookie_style: bool = field(default=False, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.2', doc_name='style: cookie', description='Cookie parameter style; earlier versions do not declare it', status='supported', client_status='supported'))
  deep_object_style: bool = field(default=True, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='style: deepObject', description='Query parameters written as `name[key]=value`; the client writes objects of scalars, and arrays, scalars, nested objects and arrays of objects, which OpenAPI leaves undefined, of query parameters and URL-encoded form members as `name[]=value`, `name=value`, `name[key][key]=value` and `name[0][key]=value` only with `--client-deep-object-brackets`', status='supported', client_status='partial'))
  structured_parameter_values: bool = field(default=True, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='Structured style values', description='Parameter and form member values that are no single scalar kind, such as nested objects, unions and `allOf`, written with a style; the client writes only a parameter outside a header, or a URL-encoded form member, that takes one item or an array of them, and with `--client-deep-object-brackets` deepObject values, as `style: deepObject` describes', status='supported', client_status='partial'))
  binary_in_text_media: bool = field(default=True, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='Binary strings in text media', description='`format: binary` strings inside a JSON, URL-encoded or text body, a parameter or a header', status='supported', client_status='not_supported'))
  multipart_media: bool = field(default=True, metadata=FeatureMetadata(introduced='OAS 3.0', doc_name='multipart media types', description='Multipart bodies; the client maps the parts of `multipart/form-data` with an object schema, or a union of objects that agree on the parts of each property, only, such as not `multipart/related`', status='supported', client_status='partial'))
